HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2243a767e564277eba4d","text":"3. **No reversal into service or enslavement.** ع ب د B004, \"making someone a slave,\" is activated by the alternation of pronouns: أَنَا against the second-person plural suffix in عَبَدتُّمْ. Mechanism: the speaker will not be reclassified as a servant/slave of the addressees' worship-system. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives enslavement under the worship root and pronoun opposition; I infer the danger of role-capture from entering the other side's object-relation. Reading change: عَابِدٌ becomes a status term the speaker refuses, not merely an action-term.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_293711edc35df1537011","text":"1. **A stable first-person role is denied against completed precedent.** Both fixed occurrences activate `ع ب د B003`, but **أَنَا عَابِدٌ** is a nominal role-predication and **عَبَدتُّمْ** is perfect. This reverses 109:3’s persons while changing the addressees’ service from imperfect to completed practice. I assign the perfect the role of gathering prior acts into precedent and the participle the role of a prospective or stable identity; the link from prior acts to precedent is inferred. The ayah therefore refuses becoming the kind of servant defined by what the addressees have already served, not merely copying a current act.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_317bd045a85ab52ae973","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:4:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]],[\"109:4:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Konuşanın isim biçimindeki kulluk rolü ile muhatapların tamamlanmış geçmiş eyleminin karşıtlığı, onların önceki uygulamasının konuşanın kalıcı kimliğine dönüşmesini reddeder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_491e704bf128824cda85","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:4:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B005\"]],[\"109:4:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Geçmişte yinelenmiş uygulama, alışkanlıkla açılmış bir yol ve devralınan emsal görünümü kazanır; konuşan bu miras yoluna girmeyi reddeder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5792b13b4b8e86e05000","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:4:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B009\"]],[\"109:4:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ortadaki ayetin kısa gecikme ya da acele çağrışımıyla zamansal bir geçiş noktası oluşturduğu önerisi, yalnızca kip karşıtlığına dayandığı için spekülatiftir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5e8495c77078da1fddf8","text":"3. **The denied role would transfer honor to their object.** Fixed `ع ب د B006` supplies honoring and magnifying. The asymmetry **عَابِدٌ / مَا عَبَدتُّمْ** assigns the speaker the potential honoring role and the addressees’ prior service its proposed object. I infer that entering the servant-role would ratify the worth their practice has conferred. The line consequently withholds recognition and magnification as well as ritual participation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_63c3a8d57068c30e62a2","text":"4. **The central ayah prevents a purely symmetric reading.** ع ب د B009, \"brief delay and slight haste,\" is only weakly activated by the temporal contrast between present participle عَابِدٌ and past عَبَدتُّمْ. Mechanism: the line looks backward and interrupts the 109:3/109:5 repetition, creating a temporal hinge. The abductive move is mine and exploratory: the packet gives a branch about short delay/non-delay, while the ayah supplies tense contrast; I infer that temporal non-coincidence matters even if the specific branch is not the main reading. Reading change: the ayah becomes a hinge between present non-overlap and prior practice, not just one more negation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001","root_000973/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_78e14101cecb6ee27a2d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:4:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"109:4:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Onların ibadet düzenine katılmak konuşanın o düzenin egemenliği altına geçirilmesi anlamına da gelebileceğinden, ayet bir statü ve sahiplik aktarımını reddeder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7d3c024df24f69dfb044","text":"1. **The speaker's own identity is denied with respect to their past practice.** The fixed ayah supplies ع ب د B003 in عَابِدٌ and عَبَدتُّمْ. The activators are the prior first-person verb in 109:2 and the second-person participle in 109:3. Mechanism: the sequence moves from \"I do not do what you do\" to \"I am not a worshipper of what you have done/worshipped.\" The abductive move is mine: the packet gives the form change and the past-tense second-person verb; I infer that past practice is being excluded from the speaker's self-description. Reading change: the ayah deepens the refusal from present action to identity across inherited or prior worship.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_80801bdd9c958cca466f","text":"Revisiting the whole packet made 109:4 the hinge of the surah. 109:3 and 109:5 are identical around it, so this central line is not another parallel denial; it interrupts the repetition with أَنَا and the past عَبَدتُّمْ. That made the \"past practice / inherited path\" reading much stronger. 109:6 then changed the past-tense object into an account-bearing order: what they worshipped is not simply past behavior but part of the dīn that remains \"for you.\" The basmalah's mercy also softened the central refusal: the speaker refuses identity-transfer without needing to erase the addressees.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_85d2a115d8b7a9649400","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:4:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B006\"]],[\"109:4:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bu kulluk rolünü üstlenmek, onların taptıklarına değer ve yücelik atfetmek anlamına da geleceğinden ayet böyle bir onay aktarımını engeller.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a9b36901a37547adf04d","text":"109:5’s exact return to the addressees’ participial clause revealed 109:4 as the central reverse test: it temporarily asks whether their completed practice can define the speaker’s durable role, then the sequence closes without convergence. In 109:6, `د ي ن B005` and `د ي ن B004` retrospectively converted that perfect from bare past reference into, respectively, established custom and a possible transfer of mastery; both arrows remain explicit inferences.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b66e57529390a78040bf","text":"2. **Past practice can be a worn customary route that the speaker will not enter.** Fixed `ع ب د B005` supplies a physically subdued and smoothed road; contextual `د ي ن B005` supplies habit and customary state. The perfect **عَبَدتُّمْ**, following the imperfect repetition in 109:2, provides accumulated practice, while **أَنَا عَابِدٌ** poses the question of durable role. I infer the material analogy that repeated passage smooths a route into custom. The denial then rejects succession into an established way, not only its object in isolation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dc09ba3be574261f0ba1","text":"4. **The refusal also blocks a transfer of mastery.** Fixed `ع ب د B001` (owned status) and `ع ب د B004` (enslaving or subjugating) are sharpened by the final `د ي ن B004` branch of subjugation and possession. The fixed switch from their completed action to the speaker’s denied identity supplies the possible transfer point. The inferred arrow is that adopting their service-object would place the speaker under its control. This changes **وَلَا أَنَا عَابِدٌ** into a refusal of acquired subject-status within their order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fd68ae434ef412184bb0","text":"2. **The speaker refuses a smoothed inheritance-path.** ع ب د B005, \"physical subduing and smoothing,\" is activated by عَبَدتُّمْ as a completed second-person practice and by د ي ن B005 \"habit and customary state\" in 109:6. Mechanism: what \"you worshipped\" appears as a path already made passable by prior use; the speaker's عَابِدٌ denies becoming a traveler on that already-smoothed road. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives past practice and the path/habit branches; I infer inheritance/path from the completed second-person action. Reading change: the ayah is not merely about an object but about refusing the authority of precedent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The nominal worshipper and the addressees' perfect worship make 109:4 a denial of durable service identity in relation to an already established practice, not only a denial of one present act.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_772bcfcbfcc825a10cfd","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
